Unparseable outputs are kept and flagged.\\n\\nLabels are built in layers:\\n- L0: gold audit of every MALLS/FOLIO held-out sentence by a cascaded 3-model panel from families disjoint from all generators and from the gemini judge (Anthropic / xAI / Zhipu). Panel-corrected gold replaces wrong gold, and the original is kept.\\n- L1: lexical-free z3 equivalence to the original and to the audited gold, up to an arity-preserving predicate and constant bijection. Bounded checking uses domains 1..4 via propositional grounding (a bounded countermodel is a sound non-equivalence); an unbounded z3 attempt follows.\\n- L2: granularity-aware equivalence (lexical, flagged).\\n- L3: full 3-model adjudication of 300 stratified candidates, with inverse-probability sampling weights. Formulas are shown as blinded A/B, so the panel is not anchored on gold.\\n- L4: gold audit of the screen's sha1-first-300 FOLIO-dev sentences (+60 buffer), plus solver re-labelling of the 3 Logic-LM screen candidates against audited gold. This gives iter 2 audited screen labels at zero extra LLM cost.\\n- L5: a label_report with wrong-gold rate, correct-but-gold-inequivalent rate, the real error-type mix (scope+cardinality share), Fleiss kappa, and Wilson CIs.\\n\\nExtras:\\n- E1: a 150-sentence entity-renamed paraphrase contamination set with renamed gold and candidates.\\n- E2: the user's EU-AI-Act pilot formulas as an unlabeled transfer split.\\n- E3: raw complexity features using the screen recipe.\\n\\nA 40-item known-label calibration gate vets the panel before any spending. The OpenRouter budget is capped at $9.3, with a ledger after every call (projected ~$8.0).\",\n  \"runpod_compute_profile\": \"cpu_plus\",\n  \"target_num_datasets\": 5,\n  \"ideal_dataset_criteria\": \"ONE merged deliverable (data_out.json, split into parts if over the file-size limit, plus mini/preview). It is organised as 4 dataset groups (splits) drawn from 5 source datasets: MALLS-v0.1-test, ProverQA-dev, FOLIO-v2-train, FOLIO-v1-validation (screen audit only) and the user's DPV/EU-AI-Act pilot.\\n\\nIDEAL PROPERTIES.\\n(1) Sentences are single NL sentences with sentence-level gold FOL from public corpora, disjoint at story level from the screen (FOLIO validation). The pool is enriched for long, heavily conditioned sentences: at least 40% of the sampled held-out sentences come from the top tercile of the composite complexity score.\\n(2) Candidates are REAL outputs of current NL->FOL systems of different families and sizes, generated under a gold-free prompt with free predicate naming. There are about 700 sentences x 9 systems = ~6,300 greedy candidates, plus 700 x 5 x 2 = 7,000 samples. Every output is present, including unparseable ones (parse_ok=false, parse_error kept).\\n(3) Labels are semantic, not string-based, and independent of the metric under test. The primary label (L1) uses NO lexical information, so it cannot share the signature's lemma aligner. Gold is audited before use, because published FOLIO/MALLS gold is ~42% wrong per arXiv:2606.02837. Adjudication is by a panel sharing no model family with any candidate generator or with the gemini judge baseline. Sampling weights are stored so population rates are unbiased.\\n(4) The class balance is usable. The target is 25-60% non-faithful among greedy candidates overall, and the dataset reports this per corpus x system x tercile.\\n(5) Documentation. Every row carries provenance (corpus, source id, gold_source in {original, panel_corrected, paper_corrected, synthetic_clean}), full label lineage (L1_orig, L1_audited, L2, L3 votes), cost, and model ids with provider. Licences are recorded: MALLS CC BY-NC 4.0, FOLIO MIT, ProverQA per its card.\\n\\nROW SCHEMA. Each row follows the aii exp_sel_data_out convention (verify with the aii-json skill before finalising). The fields are:\\n- input: a JSON string of {\\\"sentence\\\": ..., \\\"candidate_fol\\\": ...};\\n- output: final_label, one of faithful, unfaithful, unknown;\\n- metadata_* fields, flat:\\n  - fold (= split): heldout_confirm, heldout_samples, screen_gold_audit, contamination or transfer_unlabeled;\\n  - item_id = sha1(norm_sentence)[:10] + ':' + system + ':' + sample_idx;\\n  - sentence_id, corpus, corpus_subset (e.g. proverqa_hard), source_id (story_id/example_id/index);\\n  - system, provider, sample_idx (0 = greedy), temperature, raw_output, parse_ok, parse_error;\\n  - gold_fol_original, gold_fol_audited, gold_source, gold_parse_ok;\\n  - L0 fields: gold_audit_votes, gold_faithful_final, sentence_ambiguous;\\n  - L1 fields: L1_orig_status, L1_audited_status, L1_mapping, L1_entail_cand_to_gold, L1_entail_gold_to_cand, L1_method (bounded/unbounded), L1_max_domain, L1_seconds;\\n  - L2 fields: L2_status, L2_definitions, L2_lexical=true;\\n  - L3 fields: L3_selected, L3_stratum, L3_sampling_weight, L3_votes {member: {cand_faithful, gold_faithful, error_types, primary_error, ambiguous}}, L3_majority, L3_primary_error;\\n  - label_source (panel3 / equiv_audited_gold / equiv_synthetic_gold / unknown);\\n  - complexity: n_tokens, n_quantifiers, nesting_depth, n_conditions, complexity_composite, complexity_tercile, plus the z-parameters used;\\n  - gen_usd, gen_seconds.\\n\\nSIZE. Well under 300MB: ~14k rows x ~2.5KB ≈ 35MB of JSON, to be split per aii-file-size-limit.\",\n  \"dataset_search_plan\": \"Scale up in stages (aii-long-running-tasks): mini (20 sentences) -> 100 -> full. Keep a cost ledger (cost_ledger.jsonl) that is appended after EVERY OpenRouter call, using the returned usage.cost with usage accounting enabled; if that is missing, compute tokens x catalog price. HARD STOP at cumulative $9.30. Per-phase caps:\\n- generation $2.0;\\n- panel calibration $0.4;\\n- gold audits $2.6;\\n- L3 adjudication $3.0;\\n- E1 paraphrases $0.6;\\n- reserve $0.7.\\nBefore each phase, print the projected cost from the pilot's mean cost per call x the planned number of calls, and shrink the phase if the projection exceeds its cap.\\n\\nSTEP 0 — REUSE AND TOOLING (0:00-0:20).\\n- Copy the tokenizer/Pratt parser from /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_qY2a2IS-WLIs/iter_1/gen_hypo/claude_agent/feasibility/monotonicity_profile.py (read-only; copy it into src/).\\n- Extend it into src/fol_parse.py, which must:\\n  - accept ∀ ∃ ¬ ∧ ∨ → ↔ ⊕, = and ≠, and the ASCII variants (forall/exists/all/some, ~ ! not, & and, | or, ->, <->, xor, ^);\\n  - accept lowercase snake_case predicates (ProverQA), CamelCase (MALLS/FOLIO), and multi-variable binders (∀x ∀y, ∀x y);\\n  - strip code fences, trailing periods and 'FOL:' prefixes.\\n- Do NOT reuse the prototype's hash(fol)-named EnumSort: the review found it crashes when the same formula is profiled twice.\\n- Write unit tests on 15 hand formulas, including the round trip parse -> print -> parse.\\n\\nSTEP 1 — ACQUIRE SOURCES (0:20-0:50, parallel).\\n(a) CORRECTED GOLD, time-boxed to 25 minutes. The arXiv:2606.02837 v2 paper (EMNLP 2026, Brunello et al.) names https://github.com/dslab-uniud/Fixing-FOLIO-and-MALLS. That URL was 404 on 2026-09-23, and the org's other repo NL-FOL-LT does NOT contain the corrections. Try, in this order:\\n  - that URL again;\\n  - GitHub search API for 'Fixing-FOLIO' and for forks;\\n  - the arXiv source tarball https://arxiv.org/src/2606.02837 (look for JSON/CSV data or appendix tables listing corrected formulas);\\n  - HF dataset search for 'FOLIO corrected' / 'MALLS corrected' / 'dslab-uniud';\\n  - Papers-with-Code;\\n  - the EMNLP 2026 anthology attachments;\\n  - Zenodo.\\n  NOTE THE SCOPE: the paper corrected only 275 FOLIO-VALIDATION instances and the FIRST 100 of the 1,000 MALLS-test items (plus 213 GGC). So even if found, it covers (i) the screen set, which enables a human-vs-panel agreement check for L4, and (ii) at most 100 MALLS held-out items. If found, force-include all 100 MALLS items in the held-out sample with gold_source=paper_corrected, and record the release commit and URL. If not found, record the search log in gold_search_log.md and use panel-audited gold (L0).\\n(b) MALLS: hf_hub_download yuan-yang/MALLS-v0, file MALLS-v0.1-test.json (1,000 {NL, FOL} pairs; CC BY-NC 4.0). Keep the original index as source_id.\\n(c) ProverQA: hf_hub_download opendatalab/ProverQA files dev/medium.json and dev/hard.json (500 each). Do NOT use load_dataset: the repo has a schema conflict that breaks it. Each item has nl2fol, a dict mapping each context sentence to its FOL.\\n  - Keep ONLY entries whose FOL contains a quantifier or at least 2 connectives, because ground facts like 'has_x(Brecken)' are trivial.\\n  - De-template: replace person names with <NAME> and deduplicate on the normalized template.\\n  - Take at most 2 sentences per story.\\n(d) FOLIO. Train source, in priority order:\\n  - yale-nlp/FOLIO v2 (folio_v2_train.jsonl), if the HF token has gated access;\\n  - otherwise the ungated mirror tasksource/folio, train split (1,000 rows; fields story_id, premises, premises-FOL, conclusion, conclusion-FOL; premises newline-separated).\\n  Split premises and premises-FOL on newlines, and DROP any story where the counts differ (report the count). Add conclusion/conclusion-FOL as further sentences, and deduplicate sentences by normalized text (lowercase, collapse whitespace, strip final period — the SAME normalization as the screen).\\n(e) DISJOINTNESS.\\n  - Build the set of normalized sentences from BOTH FOLIO validation versions: v1 at github Yale-LILY/FOLIO data/v0.0/folio-validation.jsonl, and v2 validation from tasksource/folio or yale-nlp.\\n  - Exclude every train STORY that shares any normalized premise or conclusion with that set. Also exclude any individual sentence string that appears in it.\\n  - Assert zero overlap after filtering, and log the excluded story counts.\\n(f) SCREEN SOURCES for L4: FOLIO v1 validation from GitHub, and the Logic-LM outputs from github teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM, outputs/logic_programs/FOLIO_dev_{gpt-3.5-turbo,gpt-4,text-davinci-003}.json, where lines have the form 'FOL ::: NL'.\\n(g) USER PILOT for E2: open /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_qY2a2IS-WLIs/user_uploads/dpv_pilot_study.zip with python zipfile, READ-ONLY, extracting into the workspace under raw/pilot/. List the tree, locate every */04_fol.json and euaiact_enacting_terms.json, and inspect their structure before writing the extractor.\\n\\nSTEP 2 — GOLD VALIDITY AND COMPLEXITY (0:50-1:10).\\n- Parse every gold formula. Exclude a gold (and its sentence) if it is unparseable, unsatisfiable or valid (tautology). Check both with the bounded grounding solver below at domain sizes 1..3.\\n- Report the exclusion counts per corpus. MALLS is expected to lose a few percent to syntax errors, and 2606.02837 reports 3% syntactic errors.\\n- E3 complexity features, IDENTICAL to the screen recipe:\\n  - n_tokens: whitespace tokens of the sentence;\\n  - n_quantifiers: in the gold AST;\\n  - nesting_depth: maximum nesting of quantifier and connective nodes in the gold AST;\\n  - n_conditions: the count of the words if/unless/except/only/either/neither/not in the lowercased sentence (whole-word; count n't as not), plus the gold connective count (→ ↔ ⊕ ∨ ¬).\\n- composite = the mean of the 4 z-scores, where z is computed over the pooled eligible held-out pool; terciles are taken on the composite over that pool. Store the raw features, the means/SDs and the cut-points, so iter 2 can re-bin with the screen's parameters.\\n\\nSTEP 3 — STRATIFIED SENTENCE SAMPLE (1:10-1:20).\\n- Draw with seed 0, deterministic order by sha1(norm_sentence).\\n- Targets:\\n  - MALLS 250, including all paper-corrected items if found;\\n  - ProverQA 200 (hard 120, medium 80);\\n  - FOLIO-train 250.\\n- Within each corpus, take ≥40% (target 45%) from the top pooled tercile, 30% middle and 25% bottom. If a corpus runs short of top-tercile items, back-fill top-tercile items from the other corpora and report it.\\n- Assert: ≥40% top tercile overall, and N between 650 and 750.\\n\\nSTEP 4 — CANDIDATE GENERATION (1:20-2:30; async with aiohttp, Semaphore 12, 3 retries with backoff).\\n\\nFIRST, query the OpenRouter catalog. Confirm each id and its price, and record the provider. REQUIRED 6 systems, with the fallback in brackets:\\n- meta-llama/llama-3.1-8b-instruct;\\n- qwen/qwen-2.5-7b-instruct [qwen/qwen3-8b in non-thinking mode];\\n- mistralai/mistral-small-3.2-24b-instruct [the newest mistral-small];\\n- openai/gpt-4.1-mini;\\n- google/gemini-2.5-flash, with thinking DISABLED via reasoning {max_tokens: 0} or {enabled: false} — verify from a response that no reasoning tokens were billed;\\n- deepseek/deepseek-chat-v3.1 [deepseek/deepseek-chat-v3-0324 or v3.2], non-thinking.\\n\\nEXTRA 3 cheap systems, adding ≈$0.25 so that the system-level Kendall tau is over 9 systems rather than 6:\\n- google/gemma-3-27b-it;\\n- microsoft/phi-4;\\n- openai/gpt-oss-120b with reasoning effort low.\\nNone of the 9 may be Anthropic, xAI or Zhipu, which are reserved for the panel.\\n\\nONE frozen prompt, saved to prompts/generation_prompt.txt; its sha1 is recorded in every row:\\n'Translate the English sentence into ONE first-order logic formula.\\\\nSyntax: ∀x, ∃x, ¬, ∧, ∨, → (implies), ↔ (iff), ⊕ (exclusive or); predicates written Name(x) or Name(x, y); constants are names such as john. Choose your own predicate and constant names.\\\\nExample of the syntax only: \\\"Every bird that is not a penguin can fly.\\\" -> ∀x ((Bird(x) ∧ ¬Penguin(x)) → CanFly(x))\\\\nOutput ONLY the formula on a single line, no explanation.\\\\nSentence: {sentence}\\\\nFormula:'\\n\\nSETTINGS:\\n- Greedy: temperature 0 and max_tokens 256.\\n- Samples: gpt-4.1-mini and llama-3.1-8b at T=0.8 and top_p=1, 5 separate calls each (sample_idx 1..5), with seed=idx where the provider supports it.\\n- Extraction: take the first line containing a quantifier, a connective or '('; strip fences and a 'Formula:' prefix. Store raw_output always.\\n\\nPILOT on 20 sentences x 9 systems:\\n- If any system's parse rate is below 50% because of FORMATTING (for example fences or prose), fix the EXTRACTOR, not the prompt.\\n- If a model id fails, switch to its fallback.\\n- Freeze everything after the pilot.\\n\\nCOST: expected ≈$1.6 total, of which the gpt-4.1-mini samples are ≈$0.95. If the generation cap is threatened, cut the samples to 350 sentences, top-tercile first.\\n\\nSTEP 5 — L1 / L2 SOLVER LABELS (can start on pilot outputs at 1:40; full run 2:30-3:30; ProcessPoolExecutor with 4 workers, spawn; per-candidate wall timeout 30 s; greedy candidates before samples).\\n\\nL1 (NO lexical information):\\n- Group predicates by arity, and treat constants as a separate group. If the per-arity counts differ between candidate and gold, set L1 = non_equiv_no_bijection (this is the screen's strict definition) and send the item to L2.\\n- Otherwise enumerate bijections: the product of per-group permutations, capped at 5,040. Above the cap, try 5,040 seeded random permutations and set 'unknown_capped' if none is equivalent.\\n- For each mapping:\\n  - Ground both formulas PROPOSITIONALLY over domain {0..n-1}: expand ∀ into a conjunction and ∃ into a disjunction; predicate atoms become z3 Bools; constants range over domain elements through Int-valued choice variables, or are enumerated for n ≤ 4. Then check SAT of ¬(F↔G) with z3 for n = 1, 2, 3, 4. This is fast and avoids EnumSort naming bugs.\\n  - Prune mappings at n ≤ 2 first.\\n  - A countermodel at any n is a SOUND non-equivalence proof.\\n  - If there is none up to n = 4, attempt the UNBOUNDED check: z3 with DeclareSort and quantifiers, ¬(F↔G), timeout 10 s. The result is equiv_proved if unsat, equiv_bounded if unknown or timeout.\\n- Status ∈ {equiv_proved, equiv_bounded, non_equiv, non_equiv_no_bijection, unknown_capped, unknown_timeout, unparseable}.\\n- Also record one-directional bounded entailment (cand ⊨ gold, gold ⊨ cand) under the mapping that satisfies the most directions. This is a hint for dropped or added conditions.\\n- Run L1 against gold_fol_original (L1_orig) AND, once L0 finishes, against gold_fol_audited (L1_audited).\\n\\nL2 (granularity-aware, LEXICAL, flagged L2_lexical=true), run only when L1 is not equivalent:\\n- Split predicate names on camelCase and snake_case and lemmatize with nltk WordNet. Negation markers are not, non, un-, never, no.\\n- For each candidate predicate with no same-arity partner, try to define it as a conjunction of at most 2 gold literals (possibly negated) whose lemma set covers its lemmas, and symmetrically for gold compounds.\\n- Substitute the definition, then rerun the L1 check on the remaining predicates.\\n- Status ∈ {equiv_granular, non_equiv, not_applicable}.\\n\\nSTEP 6 — PANEL CALIBRATION GATE C0 (3:00-3:15, ≈$0.3).\\nPANEL, chosen to share NO family with any generator or with the gemini judge:\\n- M1 = anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5, non-thinking, temperature 0; budget fallback anthropic/claude-haiku-4.5;\\n- M2 = x-ai/grok-4-fast with reasoning on;\\n- M3 = z-ai/glm-4.6 [fallback moonshotai/kimi-k2-thinking].\\nConfirm the ids and prices in the catalog.\\n\\nCalibration set, 40 items with KNOWN labels, built from ProverQA dev-EASY gold (not used in the held-out set):\\n- 15 golds, faithful by construction;\\n- 15 solver-verified NON-equivalent mutants (negation flip, ∀↔∃, implication reversal, drop conjunct, argument swap, 3 each);\\n- 10 solver-verified equivalent rewrites (contrapositive, De Morgan, predicate renaming).\\nRun each member with the L3 prompt. A member must reach ≥80% accuracy; otherwise swap in its fallback and re-test once. Report the per-member accuracy in label_report.json.\\n\\nSTEP 7 — L0 HELD-OUT GOLD AUDIT and L4 SCREEN GOLD AUDIT (3:15-4:00).\\nThe prompt is saved to prompts/gold_audit.txt:\\n- The panel sees the sentence and ONE formula, with no system or source label.\\n- It returns JSON {faithful: yes/no, sentence_ambiguous: yes/no, error_types: [...], primary_error, explanation ≤ 40 words, corrected_fol (required if not faithful, in the same syntax, reusing the formula's predicate names where possible)}.\\n- The judging standard is written into the prompt: faithful = same truth conditions under a reasonable reading of the sentence; different predicate names, granularity and equivalent rewrites are acceptable; for an ambiguous sentence, faithful under any defensible reading (the ambiguity notion of 2606.02837).\\n\\nERROR TAXONOMY (shared with L3): negation_polarity, quantifier_forall_exists, implication_direction_or_only, dropped_condition, added_condition, argument_swap, conflation, wrong_split, connective_and_or, exception_misplaced, quantifier_scope, cardinality_numeric, wrong_constant, syntax_unparseable, other, none. The ∧↔∨, exception and constant types are added because the iter-1 review verified them as signature blind spots.\\n\\nCASCADE, to fit the budget:\\n- M2 and M3 vote on every item.\\n- M1 votes where they disagree, plus on a seeded random 20% (the full-panel subset used for Fleiss kappa).\\n- Final vote = M2 if M2 == M3, else M1.\\n\\nL0 SCOPE: all ~500 MALLS and FOLIO-train held-out sentences. For ProverQA, audit a 30-sentence spot check only, to test the 'synthetic = clean' assumption; if more than 10% of that check is flagged, audit all 200.\\n\\nWhen the final vote is 'not faithful':\\n- parse corrected_fol, preferring the M1 version, then M2;\\n- verify with the bounded solver that it is non-equivalent to the original and satisfiable;\\n- set gold_fol_audited = corrected and gold_source = panel_corrected.\\nIf the correction does not parse, set gold_fol_audited = null and the label for that sentence's candidates = unknown, unless L3-adjudicated.\\n\\nL4 SCREEN AUDIT:\\n- Reproduce the screen recipe: match Logic-LM NL to FOLIO v1 validation by normalized string; drop unmatched lines; drop unparseable or unsatisfiable gold; sort unique sentences by sha1(normalized text).\\n- Audit the FIRST 360 (the 300 plus a 60-item buffer), so that small parser-driven exclusion differences from the screen arms still cover their 300. Key the rows by sha1[:10].\\n- Then run L1 (solver only, $0) on the 3 Logic-LM candidates for each audited sentence against the audited gold. Store the results as split screen_gold_audit, so iter 2 gets audited screen labels directly.\\n- If the 2606.02837 corrections were found, report the panel-vs-human agreement on the overlapping FOLIO-validation items. This is the only direct validity check of the panel against human relabelling.\\n\\nSTEP 8 — L3 CANDIDATE ADJUDICATION (4:00-4:45, ≈$2.9).\\n\\nSAMPLE (seed 0), 300 greedy candidates:\\n- 250 L1_audited non-equivalent (including no_bijection and every L2 equiv_granular item, up to 40), stratified corpus(3) x system-tier(required/extra) x tercile(3), with the top tercile oversampled 1.5x;\\n- 50 L1_audited equivalent (proved or bounded), to catch agreement with wrong gold and to test the solver labels.\\nStore L3_sampling_weight = 1 / inclusion probability.\\n\\nPROMPT (prompts/adjudication.txt), BLINDED and SYMMETRIC:\\n- The panel sees the sentence and 'Formula A' and 'Formula B' (gold and candidate in seeded random order). It is NOT told which is the reference, nor any system name.\\n- It judges EACH formula independently against the sentence, and also says whether A and B mean the same thing (a cross-check against the solver).\\n- Output JSON per formula: {faithful, error_types, primary_error}, plus {sentence_ambiguous, same_meaning}.\\n- All 3 members vote on all 300 items (full Fleiss kappa). The label is the majority. Record the dissent pattern, and also the leave-one-out majority for items where the M-member's family could be implicated. Expected to be none, given family-disjointness; state that in the README.\\n\\nSTEP 9 — FINAL LABEL (output field). Precedence:\\n- (1) L3 majority, if adjudicated (label_source = panel3);\\n- (2) else, ProverQA: L1_orig vs synthetic gold (equiv_* -> faithful, non_equiv* -> unfaithful; label_source = equiv_synthetic_gold);\\n- (3) else, L1_audited vs audited gold (equiv_proved / equiv_bounded -> faithful; non_equiv / non_equiv_no_bijection -> unfaithful; label_source = equiv_audited_gold);\\n- (4) else unknown.\\nUnknown items are KEPT and counted; they are excluded only from label-requiring statistics.\\nL2 equiv_granular items not adjudicated keep the L1-based label, but carry L2_status so iter 2 can run a sensitivity analysis with 'L2 counts as faithful'.\\nSamples (heldout_samples split) get L1 labels only.\\n\\nSTEP 10 — E1 CONTAMINATION SET (4:45-5:10, ≈$0.4).\\n- Select 150 held-out sentences (75 FOLIO-train, 75 MALLS) whose audited gold contains at least one constant or named entity, preferring panel-faithful gold, top tercile first.\\n- Paraphrase with openai/gpt-4.1-mini at T=0.7. The call must reword the sentence AND rename every named entity to a fresh name from a fixed list (seeded), and return JSON {paraphrase, rename_map}.\\n- Apply rename_map to the gold constants, and to entity substrings inside predicate names (for example LivesInParis -> LivesInOslo). Re-parse.\\n- Verify with M2 (grok-4-fast): (i) same meaning up to renaming? (ii) does the renamed gold faithfully formalize the paraphrase? Keep only pairs where both answers are yes; target ≥120 kept.\\n- Carry the 9 greedy candidates of the original sentence, with constants renamed by case-insensitive match (unmatched -> contamination_rename_incomplete flag). Their final_label transfers from the original.\\n- Also store the original sentence id, so the iter-2 gold-recall probe can prompt with the original text.\\n\\nSTEP 11 — E2 TRANSFER (5:10-5:25, $0).\\n- One row per formula in each 04_fol.json (definition x condition x run).\\n- input.sentence = the definition text from euaiact_enacting_terms.json.\\n- metadata: definition_id, condition, run, and any pilot structural-metric fields present.\\n- output = 'unlabeled'.\\n- Non-FOL constructs such as ⊆ between variables: parse_ok = false, kept.\\n\\nSTEP 12 — ASSEMBLE AND VALIDATE (5:25-6:00).\\n- Write data_out.json with groups heldout_confirm, heldout_samples, screen_gold_audit, contamination and transfer_unlabeled. Validate the schema with aii-json, split with aii-file-size-limit, and make mini and preview files.\\n- label_report.json (documentation of label quality, not an analysis):\\n  - counts per corpus x system x tercile, and exclusion counts;\\n  - parse rate per system, and the L1 status distribution;\\n  - wrong-gold rate per corpus (L0 panel-unfaithful share, with Wilson 95% CIs);\\n  - correct-but-gold-inequivalent rate = weighted share of L3-faithful among L1_orig non-equivalent, and among L1_audited non-equivalent, with Wilson/bootstrap CIs;\\n  - the error-type distribution among L3-unfaithful items, weighted, with the scope + cardinality share explicitly;\\n  - solver-vs-panel agreement on the 50 equivalent items and on same_meaning;\\n  - Fleiss kappa (L3 all items; L0 on the 20% full-panel subset), per-member calibration accuracy, and the total $ per phase.\\n- README.md and .aii/manifest.yaml: keep data_out*, label_report.json, raw generations (raw/generations/*.jsonl — irreproducible API outputs) and cost_ledger.jsonl; delete raw/hf_downloads/ as redownloadable (with the source hf ids and URLs).\\n\\nFAILURE HANDLING.\\n- Budget pressure, cut in this order: samples to 350 sentences -> M1 swapped to claude-haiku-4.5 -> L3 to 200 (170 non-equiv + 30 equiv) -> E1 to 100. Never cut L0/L4.\\n- Solver slowness: label greedy candidates first; samples are best-effort (report coverage).\\n- If a panel member fails calibration twice, use a 2-member panel plus M1 on everything, and report it.\\n- If the FOLIO train mirror is unavailable, use the Yale-LILY GitHub v0.0 folio-train.jsonl.\\n- If the disjoint FOLIO pool has fewer than 250 eligible sentences, take all of them and back-fill from MALLS.\",\n  \"domain_practice\": \"READ: the aii neuro-symbolic handbook (mid-2026) and arXiv:2606.02837 v2 (Fixing FOLIO and MALLS: scope of the correction, error categories, release URL). Also: the HF cards of MALLS-v0 (yuan-yang), ProverQA (opendatalab) and the FOLIO mirrors (yale-nlp gated; tasksource/folio ungated, 1,000 train / 203 validation); the abstract of GenV arXiv:2609.11085; the org repos of dslab-uniud (NL-FOL-LT has perturbations, not corrections); and this run's strategy, review and hypothesis files.\\n\\nHOW DATASETS OF THIS KIND ARE BUILT IN THIS FIELD.\\n\\n(1) DATA.\\n- FOLIO (Han et al. 2022; v2 2024) and MALLS (Yang et al. 2023; 1k 'human-verified' test) are the standard sentence-level NL->FOL corpora.\\n- Since 2606.02837 (EMNLP 2026), BOTH are known to carry ~42% wrong gold: 42.5% of FOLIO-validation and 42% of MALLS-test entries, with ~32% / ~39% semantic errors and 10.5% / 3% syntactic. The paper also records 17.8% / 51% ambiguous NL.\\n- The corrections cover only 275 FOLIO-validation instances and the first 100 MALLS-test items. The release repo named in the paper returns 404 as of 2026-09-23.\\n- ProverQA (ICLR 2025) is the accepted 'clean, contamination-resistant' prover-synthesized set. Its nl2fol dicts give sentence-level pairs, but many are trivial ground facts and the language is templated.\\n- The handbook's critical rule: never score on shipped FOLIO/MALLS gold without saying so; use corrected or prover-synthesized gold.\\n- Saturation: sentence-level translation is 'largely handled' (handbook S23), so errors of current systems on short sentences are rare. Long, conditioned sentences are where the errors are.\\n\\n(2) LABELS.\\n- The accepted correctness notion is semantic: prover or solver equivalence to gold (LINC/Logic-LM lineage; the logical-equivalence 'LE' metric; GenV distils exactly such a Z3-equivalence oracle to 0.961 AUROC). It is computed up to predicate renaming, because vocabulary is free.\\n- Bounded model checks are accepted only as refutations (a countermodel); bounded 'equivalence' must be reported as such.\\n- Human or LLM re-annotation with explicit ambiguity handling is the 2026 norm (2606.02837 used categories incorrect-syntactic, incorrect-semantic and ambiguous).\\n- LLM panels are accepted when members are family-disjoint from the systems judged, calibrated on known-label items, and reported with inter-rater agreement (Fleiss kappa) and dissent.\\n\\n(3) CANDIDATES.\\n- Meta-evaluations use REAL outputs of several heterogeneous systems. Perturbations are mechanism evidence only (Thatikonda et al., EMNLP-F 2025).\\n- Unparseable outputs are counted, never dropped.\\n- The field increasingly distrusts 2023-era system outputs as unrepresentative of current error mixes.\\n\\n(4) HOW MUCH IS ENOUGH.\\n- Item-level AUROC on ~1,000+ candidates gives CI half-widths of about ±0.03; per complexity tercile, a few hundred items each.\\n- Rate estimates from ~300 adjudicated items give Wilson half-widths of about ±5.7pp at p = 0.5.\\n- System-level rank correlation is weak with few systems. With 6 systems, a Kendall tau of 0.6 has a one-sided permutation p of about 0.07; with 9 systems about 0.01. More systems is the fix.\\n\\n(5) REPORTING AND DOCUMENTATION.\\n- Provenance per item, including gold_source.\\n- Licences: MALLS CC BY-NC 4.0 (GPT-4-generated); FOLIO MIT.\\n- Exclusion counts at every filter.\\n- Sampling weights for any stratified adjudication, so population rates are unbiased.\\n- Both labelings (original versus audited gold), to expose label bias.\\n- A contamination check via entity-renamed paraphrases, because FOLIO and MALLS are public and in pretraining data.\",\n  \"practice_alignment\": \"MEETS:\\n- (1) Corpora: the standard ones (MALLS, FOLIO, ProverQA). Gold is audited before use (L0), and paper-corrected gold is used where retrievable. gold_source is recorded per row, and both L1_orig and L1_audited are kept, so the label bias can be reported, as the handbook rule requires.\\n- (2) Primary labels are solver equivalence up to an arity-preserving bijection, with NO lexical information. That makes them independent of the signature's aligner, which is the reviewer's circularity concern. Bounded results are reported as bounded; only countermodels count as proofs of non-equivalence; an unbounded z3 attempt upgrades equivalences.\\n- (3) Real outputs from 9 current systems across 7 families and sizes from 8B to frontier-mini. Unparseable outputs are kept.\\n- (4) The adjudication panel is family-disjoint from every generator and from the gemini judge. It passes a known-label calibration gate, reports Fleiss kappa, and sees the formulas BLINDED and symmetrically (A/B, reference not identified), so it is not anchored on possibly wrong gold. Ambiguity is recorded, following 2606.02837's categories.\\n- (5) Sampling weights are stored, and rates carry CIs. The complexity oversampling (≥40% top tercile) matches the user's priority and the field's saturation warning. Story-level disjointness from the screen is asserted.\\n\\nDEPARTURES, each with its justification and cost:\\n- (a) The corrected gold is probably unavailable: the repo is 404, and even when released it covers only 100 MALLS items and FOLIO-VALIDATION. Most held-out gold is therefore LLM-panel-audited, not human-corrected. Justified: there is no human budget, and the paper's own framework is itself LLM-assisted. Cost: panel errors propagate into labels.\\n  Mitigations: the calibration gate; blinded A/B adjudication; a solver cross-check on 50 equivalent items; a human-vs-panel agreement check if the corrections surface; and both labelings released.\\n- (b) Only ~300 candidates get full 3-model adjudication, while the other ~6k greedy labels are solver equivalence to audited gold. The latter still mislabel correct-but-inequivalent candidates as wrong. Justified: budget (~$0.01 per full-panel item). Cost: residual label noise on non-adjudicated items; its RATE is estimated from L3 with weights, and L2 flags the granular cases for sensitivity analysis.\\n- (c) The generation prompt carries ONE generic syntax illustration, so it is not strictly zero-shot. Justified: without it, 8B models emit prose and markdown, which inflates 'unparseable' for formatting rather than for meaning. Cost: slight prompt bias toward FOLIO style; the example is dataset-external and identical for all systems.\\n- (d) 9 systems instead of the direction's 6. The 3 cheap extras cost about $0.25 and raise system-level tau power (p ≈ 0.07 -> 0.01 at tau = 0.6). No cost to credibility; the 6 required systems remain reportable on their own.\\n- (e) A gold audit (L0) on all held-out MALLS and FOLIO sentences was not in the direction. It is added because, with ~42% wrong gold, equivalence-to-original labels would be near-useless as confirmation ground truth. It is paid for by a cascade (2 cheap members, the frontier member only on disagreements plus 20%). Cost: kappa is computed only on the 20% full-panel subset for L0.\\n- (f) ProverQA gold is trusted as synthetic-clean after a 30-item spot check rather than a full audit. Cost: small; the audit escalates if more than 10% is flagged. ProverQA's templated language makes it the easiest corpus, and the result will be reported per corpus.\\n- (g) The screen audit covers sha1-first-360 rather than exactly the arms' 300, because the arms' parsers may exclude slightly different golds. Rows are keyed by sha1[:10] for the join. Cost: $0.3.\\n- (h) Contamination: entity renaming plus paraphrase, verified by one panel member (M2), not by the full panel. Cost: a few paraphrases may drift in meaning; mitigated by requiring both checks and keeping at least 120 items.\\n- (i) Temperature-0 'greedy' output is not bit-deterministic across OpenRouter providers. The provider is recorded per row. Cost: exact regeneration may differ; the raw outputs are kept as the ground truth of what was scored.\\n\\nUNFIXABLE WITHIN THIS ARTIFACT: no human annotation, and the panel's own blind spots. A reviewer will ask how good the panel is on real (not synthetic) errors; only the 2606.02837 overlap, if retrievable, answers that directly.\",\n  \"builds_on\": \"Iteration 1: the sibling artifacts (screen Arms A and B) run in PARALLEL and have not produced anything yet, and this direction declares no dependencies. So this dataset is self-contained and reproduces the shared recipes rather than reading them.\\n\\nREUSED:\\n- (1) The FOL tokenizer and Pratt parser in the GEN_HYPO feasibility prototype: /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_qY2a2IS-WLIs/iter_1/gen_hypo/claude_agent/feasibility/monotonicity_profile.py (and role_anchoring.py). Copy it into src/ and extend it for ASCII, snake_case and multi-binder syntax. Do NOT reuse its hash-named EnumSort: the iter-1 review (review_hypo/probes) showed it crashes on repeated formulas, hence the propositional-grounding solver in this plan.\\n- (2) The review's findings (review_hypo/probes/blindspot_probe.py, relativized_probe.py): ∧↔∨, exception-consequent swaps and swapped constants are signature blind spots. The adjudication taxonomy therefore adds connective_and_or, exception_misplaced and wrong_constant, so iter 2 can measure how often these real errors occur.\\n- (3) The strategy's frozen screen recipe (gen_strat_1/.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json: Logic-LM FOLIO_dev outputs, normalized-string matching, sha1-sorted first 300, item_id = sha1[:10]:system, the complexity-feature definitions). It is reproduced EXACTLY so that L4 audited labels join the arms' screen_scores.jsonl by key. A 60-item buffer absorbs parser-level exclusion differences.\\n- (4) The GEN_HYPO probe finding that flash-lite fails downward probes, while gemini-2.5-flash and gpt-4.1-mini are adequate. This is why no lite models serve as panel members.\\n- (5) The user's pilot zip (user_uploads/dpv_pilot_study.zip) becomes the E2 transfer split.\\n\\nIf the screen arms finish before this artifact's L4 step, the executor MAY read their screen_set.json from /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_qY2a2IS-WLIs/3_invention_loop/iter_1/gen_art/ (search for screen_set.json) to take the arms' exact 300 ids. Per-phase caps:\\n- generation $2.0;\\n- panel calibration $0.4;\\n- gold audits $2.6;\\n- L3 adjudication $3.0;\\n- E1 paraphrases $0.6;\\n- reserve $0.7.\\nBefore each phase, print the projected cost from the pilot's mean cost per call x the planned number of calls, and shrink the phase if the projection exceeds its cap.\\n\\nSTEP 0 — REUSE AND TOOLING (0:00-0:20).\\n- Copy the tokenizer/Pratt parser from /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_qY2a2IS-WLIs/iter_1/gen_hypo/claude_agent/feasibility/monotonicity_profile.py (read-only; copy it into src/).\\n- Extend it into src/fol_parse.py, which must:\\n  - accept ∀ ∃ ¬ ∧ ∨ → ↔ ⊕, = and ≠, and the ASCII variants (forall/exists/all/some, ~ ! not, & and, | or, ->, <->, xor, ^);\\n  - accept lowercase snake_case predicates (ProverQA), CamelCase (MALLS/FOLIO), and multi-variable binders (∀x ∀y, ∀x y);\\n  - strip code fences, trailing periods and 'FOL:' prefixes.\\n- Do NOT reuse the prototype's hash(fol)-named EnumSort: the review found it crashes when the same formula is profiled twice.\\n- Write unit tests on 15 hand formulas, including the round trip parse -> print -> parse.\\n\\nSTEP 1 — ACQUIRE SOURCES (0:20-0:50, parallel).\\n(a) CORRECTED GOLD, time-boxed to 25 minutes. The arXiv:2606.02837 v2 paper (EMNLP 2026, Brunello et al.) names https://github.com/dslab-uniud/Fixing-FOLIO-and-MALLS. That URL was 404 on 2026-09-23, and the org's other repo NL-FOL-LT does NOT contain the corrections. Try, in this order:\\n  - that URL again;\\n  - GitHub search API for 'Fixing-FOLIO' and for forks;\\n  - the arXiv source tarball https://arxiv.org/src/2606.02837 (look for JSON/CSV data or appendix tables listing corrected formulas);\\n  - HF dataset search for 'FOLIO corrected' / 'MALLS corrected' / 'dslab-uniud';\\n  - Papers-with-Code;\\n  - the EMNLP 2026 anthology attachments;\\n  - Zenodo.\\n  NOTE THE SCOPE: the paper corrected only 275 FOLIO-VALIDATION instances and the FIRST 100 of the 1,000 MALLS-test items (plus 213 GGC). So even if found, it covers (i) the screen set, which enables a human-vs-panel agreement check for L4, and (ii) at most 100 MALLS held-out items. If found, force-include all 100 MALLS items in the held-out sample with gold_source=paper_corrected, and record the release commit and URL. If not found, record the search log in gold_search_log.md and use panel-audited gold (L0).\\n(b) MALLS: hf_hub_download yuan-yang/MALLS-v0, file MALLS-v0.1-test.json (1,000 {NL, FOL} pairs; CC BY-NC 4.0). Keep the original index as source_id.\\n(c) ProverQA: hf_hub_download opendatalab/ProverQA files dev/medium.json and dev/hard.json (500 each). Do NOT use load_dataset: the repo has a schema conflict that breaks it. Each item has nl2fol, a dict mapping each context sentence to its FOL.\\n  - Keep ONLY entries whose FOL contains a quantifier or at least 2 connectives, because ground facts like 'has_x(Brecken)' are trivial.\\n  - De-template: replace person names with <NAME> and deduplicate on the normalized template.\\n  - Take at most 2 sentences per story.\\n(d) FOLIO. Train source, in priority order:\\n  - yale-nlp/FOLIO v2 (folio_v2_train.jsonl), if the HF token has gated access;\\n  - otherwise the ungated mirror tasksource/folio, train split (1,000 rows; fields story_id, premises, premises-FOL, conclusion, conclusion-FOL; premises newline-separated).\\n  Split premises and premises-FOL on newlines, and DROP any story where the counts differ (report the count). Add conclusion/conclusion-FOL as further sentences, and deduplicate sentences by normalized text (lowercase, collapse whitespace, strip final period — the SAME normalization as the screen).\\n(e) DISJOINTNESS.\\n  - Build the set of normalized sentences from BOTH FOLIO validation versions: v1 at github Yale-LILY/FOLIO data/v0.0/folio-validation.jsonl, and v2 validation from tasksource/folio or yale-nlp.\\n  - Exclude every train STORY that shares any normalized premise or conclusion with that set. Also exclude any individual sentence string that appears in it.\\n  - Assert zero overlap after filtering, and log the excluded story counts.\\n(f) SCREEN SOURCES for L4: FOLIO v1 validation from GitHub, and the Logic-LM outputs from github teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM, outputs/logic_programs/FOLIO_dev_{gpt-3.5-turbo,gpt-4,text-davinci-003}.json, where lines have the form 'FOL ::: NL'.\\n(g) USER PILOT for E2: open /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_qY2a2IS-WLIs/user_uploads/dpv_pilot_study.zip with python zipfile, READ-ONLY, extracting into the workspace under raw/pilot/. List the tree, locate every */04_fol.json and euaiact_enacting_terms.json, and inspect their structure before writing the extractor.\\n\\nSTEP 2 — GOLD VALIDITY AND COMPLEXITY (0:50-1:10).\\n- Parse every gold formula. Exclude a gold (and its sentence) if it is unparseable, unsatisfiable or valid (tautology). Check both with the bounded grounding solver below at domain sizes 1..3.\\n- Report the exclusion counts per corpus. MALLS is expected to lose a few percent to syntax errors, and 2606.02837 reports 3% syntactic errors.\\n- E3 complexity features, IDENTICAL to the screen recipe:\\n  - n_tokens: whitespace tokens of the sentence;\\n  - n_quantifiers: in the gold AST;\\n  - nesting_depth: maximum nesting of quantifier and connective nodes in the gold AST;\\n  - n_conditions: the count of the words if/unless/except/only/either/neither/not in the lowercased sentence (whole-word; count n't as not), plus the gold connective count (→ ↔ ⊕ ∨ ¬).\\n- composite = the mean of the 4 z-scores, where z is computed over the pooled eligible held-out pool; terciles are taken on the composite over that pool. Store the raw features, the means/SDs and the cut-points, so iter 2 can re-bin with the screen's parameters.\\n\\nSTEP 3 — STRATIFIED SENTENCE SAMPLE (1:10-1:20).\\n- Draw with seed 0, deterministic order by sha1(norm_sentence).\\n- Targets:\\n  - MALLS 250, including all paper-corrected items if found;\\n  - ProverQA 200 (hard 120, medium 80);\\n  - FOLIO-train 250.\\n- Within each corpus, take ≥40% (target 45%) from the top pooled tercile, 30% middle and 25% bottom. If a corpus runs short of top-tercile items, back-fill top-tercile items from the other corpora and report it.\\n- Assert: ≥40% top tercile overall, and N between 650 and 750.\\n\\nSTEP 4 — CANDIDATE GENERATION (1:20-2:30; async with aiohttp, Semaphore 12, 3 retries with backoff).\\n\\nFIRST, query the OpenRouter catalog. Confirm each id and its price, and record the provider. REQUIRED 6 systems, with the fallback in brackets:\\n- meta-llama/llama-3.1-8b-instruct;\\n- qwen/qwen-2.5-7b-instruct [qwen/qwen3-8b in non-thinking mode];\\n- mistralai/mistral-small-3.2-24b-instruct [the newest mistral-small];\\n- openai/gpt-4.1-mini;\\n- google/gemini-2.5-flash, with thinking DISABLED via reasoning {max_tokens: 0} or {enabled: false} — verify from a response that no reasoning tokens were billed;\\n- deepseek/deepseek-chat-v3.1 [deepseek/deepseek-chat-v3-0324 or v3.2], non-thinking.\\n\\nEXTRA 3 cheap systems, adding ≈$0.25 so that the system-level Kendall tau is over 9 systems rather than 6:\\n- google/gemma-3-27b-it;\\n- microsoft/phi-4;\\n- openai/gpt-oss-120b with reasoning effort low.\\nNone of the 9 may be Anthropic, xAI or Zhipu, which are reserved for the panel.\\n\\nONE frozen prompt, saved to prompts/generation_prompt.txt; its sha1 is recorded in every row:\\n'Translate the English sentence into ONE first-order logic formula.\\\\nSyntax: ∀x, ∃x, ¬, ∧, ∨, → (implies), ↔ (iff), ⊕ (exclusive or); predicates written Name(x) or Name(x, y); constants are names such as john. Choose your own predicate and constant names.\\\\nExample of the syntax only: \\\"Every bird that is not a penguin can fly.\\\" -> ∀x ((Bird(x) ∧ ¬Penguin(x)) → CanFly(x))\\\\nOutput ONLY the formula on a single line, no explanation.\\\\nSentence: {sentence}\\\\nFormula:'\\n\\nSETTINGS:\\n- Greedy: temperature 0 and max_tokens 256.\\n- Samples: gpt-4.1-mini and llama-3.1-8b at T=0.8 and top_p=1, 5 separate calls each (sample_idx 1..5), with seed=idx where the provider supports it.\\n- Extraction: take the first line containing a quantifier, a connective or '('; strip fences and a 'Formula:' prefix. Store raw_output always.\\n\\nPILOT on 20 sentences x 9 systems:\\n- If any system's parse rate is below 50% because of FORMATTING (for example fences or prose), fix the EXTRACTOR, not the prompt.\\n- If a model id fails, switch to its fallback.\\n- Freeze everything after the pilot.\\n\\nCOST: expected ≈$1.6 total, of which the gpt-4.1-mini samples are ≈$0.95. If the generation cap is threatened, cut the samples to 350 sentences, top-tercile first.\\n\\nSTEP 5 — L1 / L2 SOLVER LABELS (can start on pilot outputs at 1:40; full run 2:30-3:30; ProcessPoolExecutor with 4 workers, spawn; per-candidate wall timeout 30 s; greedy candidates before samples).\\n\\nL1 (NO lexical information):\\n- Group predicates by arity, and treat constants as a separate group. If the per-arity counts differ between candidate and gold, set L1 = non_equiv_no_bijection (this is the screen's strict definition) and send the item to L2.\\n- Otherwise enumerate bijections: the product of per-group permutations, capped at 5,040. Above the cap, try 5,040 seeded random permutations and set 'unknown_capped' if none is equivalent.\\n- For each mapping:\\n  - Ground both formulas PROPOSITIONALLY over domain {0..n-1}: expand ∀ into a conjunction and ∃ into a disjunction; predicate atoms become z3 Bools; constants range over domain elements through Int-valued choice variables, or are enumerated for n ≤ 4. Then check SAT of ¬(F↔G) with z3 for n = 1, 2, 3, 4. This is fast and avoids EnumSort naming bugs.\\n  - Prune mappings at n ≤ 2 first.\\n  - A countermodel at any n is a SOUND non-equivalence proof.\\n  - If there is none up to n = 4, attempt the UNBOUNDED check: z3 with DeclareSort and quantifiers, ¬(F↔G), timeout 10 s. The result is equiv_proved if unsat, equiv_bounded if unknown or timeout.\\n- Status ∈ {equiv_proved, equiv_bounded, non_equiv, non_equiv_no_bijection, unknown_capped, unknown_timeout, unparseable}.\\n- Also record one-directional bounded entailment (cand ⊨ gold, gold ⊨ cand) under the mapping that satisfies the most directions. This is a hint for dropped or added conditions.\\n- Run L1 against gold_fol_original (L1_orig) AND, once L0 finishes, against gold_fol_audited (L1_audited).\\n\\nL2 (granularity-aware, LEXICAL, flagged L2_lexical=true), run only when L1 is not equivalent:\\n- Split predicate names on camelCase and snake_case and lemmatize with nltk WordNet. Negation markers are not, non, un-, never, no.\\n- For each candidate predicate with no same-arity partner, try to define it as a conjunction of at most 2 gold literals (possibly negated) whose lemma set covers its lemmas, and symmetrically for gold compounds.\\n- Substitute the definition, then rerun the L1 check on the remaining predicates.\\n- Status ∈ {equiv_granular, non_equiv, not_applicable}.\\n\\nSTEP 6 — PANEL CALIBRATION GATE C0 (3:00-3:15, ≈$0.3).\\nPANEL, chosen to share NO family with any generator or with the gemini judge:\\n- M1 = anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5, non-thinking, temperature 0; budget fallback anthropic/claude-haiku-4.5;\\n- M2 = x-ai/grok-4-fast with reasoning on;\\n- M3 = z-ai/glm-4.6 [fallback moonshotai/kimi-k2-thinking].\\nConfirm the ids and prices in the catalog.\\n\\nCalibration set, 40 items with KNOWN labels, built from ProverQA dev-EASY gold (not used in the held-out set):\\n- 15 golds, faithful by construction;\\n- 15 solver-verified NON-equivalent mutants (negation flip, ∀↔∃, implication reversal, drop conjunct, argument swap, 3 each);\\n- 10 solver-verified equivalent rewrites (contrapositive, De Morgan, predicate renaming).\\nRun each member with the L3 prompt. A member must reach ≥80% accuracy; otherwise swap in its fallback and re-test once. Report the per-member accuracy in label_report.json.\\n\\nSTEP 7 — L0 HELD-OUT GOLD AUDIT and L4 SCREEN GOLD AUDIT (3:15-4:00).\\nThe prompt is saved to prompts/gold_audit.txt:\\n- The panel sees the sentence and ONE formula, with no system or source label.\\n- It returns JSON {faithful: yes/no, sentence_ambiguous: yes/no, error_types: [...], primary_error, explanation ≤ 40 words, corrected_fol (required if not faithful, in the same syntax, reusing the formula's predicate names where possible)}.\\n- The judging standard is written into the prompt: faithful = same truth conditions under a reasonable reading of the sentence; different predicate names, granularity and equivalent rewrites are acceptable; for an ambiguous sentence, faithful under any defensible reading (the ambiguity notion of 2606.02837).\\n\\nERROR TAXONOMY (shared with L3): negation_polarity, quantifier_forall_exists, implication_direction_or_only, dropped_condition, added_condition, argument_swap, conflation, wrong_split, connective_and_or, exception_misplaced, quantifier_scope, cardinality_numeric, wrong_constant, syntax_unparseable, other, none. The ∧↔∨, exception and constant types are added because the iter-1 review verified them as signature blind spots.\\n\\nCASCADE, to fit the budget:\\n- M2 and M3 vote on every item.\\n- M1 votes where they disagree, plus on a seeded random 20% (the full-panel subset used for Fleiss kappa).\\n- Final vote = M2 if M2 == M3, else M1.\\n\\nL0 SCOPE: all ~500 MALLS and FOLIO-train held-out sentences. For ProverQA, audit a 30-sentence spot check only, to test the 'synthetic = clean' assumption; if more than 10% of that check is flagged, audit all 200.\\n\\nWhen the final vote is 'not faithful':\\n- parse corrected_fol, preferring the M1 version, then M2;\\n- verify with the bounded solver that it is non-equivalent to the original and satisfiable;\\n- set gold_fol_audited = corrected and gold_source = panel_corrected.\\nIf the correction does not parse, set gold_fol_audited = null and the label for that sentence's candidates = unknown, unless L3-adjudicated.\\n\\nL4 SCREEN AUDIT:\\n- Reproduce the screen recipe: match Logic-LM NL to FOLIO v1 validation by normalized string; drop unmatched lines; drop unparseable or unsatisfiable gold; sort unique sentences by sha1(normalized text).\\n- Audit the FIRST 360 (the 300 plus a 60-item buffer), so that small parser-driven exclusion differences from the screen arms still cover their 300. Key the rows by sha1[:10].\\n- Then run L1 (solver only, $0) on the 3 Logic-LM candidates for each audited sentence against the audited gold. Store the results as split screen_gold_audit, so iter 2 gets audited screen labels directly.\\n- If the 2606.02837 corrections were found, report the panel-vs-human agreement on the overlapping FOLIO-validation items. This is the only direct validity check of the panel against human relabelling.\\n\\nSTEP 8 — L3 CANDIDATE ADJUDICATION (4:00-4:45, ≈$2.9).\\n\\nSAMPLE (seed 0), 300 greedy candidates:\\n- 250 L1_audited non-equivalent (including no_bijection and every L2 equiv_granular item, up to 40), stratified corpus(3) x system-tier(required/extra) x tercile(3), with the top tercile oversampled 1.5x;\\n- 50 L1_audited equivalent (proved or bounded), to catch agreement with wrong gold and to test the solver labels.\\nStore L3_sampling_weight = 1 / inclusion probability.\\n\\nPROMPT (prompts/adjudication.txt), BLINDED and SYMMETRIC:\\n- The panel sees the sentence and 'Formula A' and 'Formula B' (gold and candidate in seeded random order). It is NOT told which is the reference, nor any system name.\\n- It judges EACH formula independently against the sentence, and also says whether A and B mean the same thing (a cross-check against the solver).\\n- Output JSON per formula: {faithful, error_types, primary_error}, plus {sentence_ambiguous, same_meaning}.\\n- All 3 members vote on all 300 items (full Fleiss kappa). The label is the majority. Record the dissent pattern, and also the leave-one-out majority for items where the M-member's family could be implicated. Expected to be none, given family-disjointness; state that in the README.\\n\\nSTEP 9 — FINAL LABEL (output field). Precedence:\\n- (1) L3 majority, if adjudicated (label_source = panel3);\\n- (2) else, ProverQA: L1_orig vs synthetic gold (equiv_* -> faithful, non_equiv* -> unfaithful; label_source = equiv_synthetic_gold);\\n- (3) else, L1_audited vs audited gold (equiv_proved / equiv_bounded -> faithful; non_equiv / non_equiv_no_bijection -> unfaithful; label_source = equiv_audited_gold);\\n- (4) else unknown.\\nUnknown items are KEPT and counted; they are excluded only from label-requiring statistics.\\nL2 equiv_granular items not adjudicated keep the L1-based label, but carry L2_status so iter 2 can run a sensitivity analysis with 'L2 counts as faithful'.\\nSamples (heldout_samples split) get L1 labels only.\\n\\nSTEP 10 — E1 CONTAMINATION SET (4:45-5:10, ≈$0.4).\\n- Select 150 held-out sentences (75 FOLIO-train, 75 MALLS) whose audited gold contains at least one constant or named entity, preferring panel-faithful gold, top tercile first.\\n- Paraphrase with openai/gpt-4.1-mini at T=0.7. The call must reword the sentence AND rename every named entity to a fresh name from a fixed list (seeded), and return JSON {paraphrase, rename_map}.\\n- Apply rename_map to the gold constants, and to entity substrings inside predicate names (for example LivesInParis -> LivesInOslo). Re-parse.\\n- Verify with M2 (grok-4-fast): (i) same meaning up to renaming? (ii) does the renamed gold faithfully formalize the paraphrase? Keep only pairs where both answers are yes; target ≥120 kept.\\n- Carry the 9 greedy candidates of the original sentence, with constants renamed by case-insensitive match (unmatched -> contamination_rename_incomplete flag). Their final_label transfers from the original.\\n- Also store the original sentence id, so the iter-2 gold-recall probe can prompt with the original text.\\n\\nSTEP 11 — E2 TRANSFER (5:10-5:25, $0).\\n- One row per formula in each 04_fol.json (definition x condition x run).\\n- input.sentence = the definition text from euaiact_enacting_terms.json.\\n- metadata: definition_id, condition, run, and any pilot structural-metric fields present.\\n- output = 'unlabeled'.\\n- Non-FOL constructs such as ⊆ between variables: parse_ok = false, kept.\\n\\nSTEP 12 — ASSEMBLE AND VALIDATE (5:25-6:00).\\n- Write data_out.json with groups heldout_confirm, heldout_samples, screen_gold_audit, contamination and transfer_unlabeled. Validate the schema with aii-json, split with aii-file-size-limit, and make mini and preview files.\\n- label_report.json (documentation of label quality, not an analysis):\\n  - counts per corpus x system x tercile, and exclusion counts;\\n  - parse rate per system, and the L1 status distribution;\\n  - wrong-gold rate per corpus (L0 panel-unfaithful share, with Wilson 95% CIs);\\n  - correct-but-gold-inequivalent rate = weighted share of L3-faithful among L1_orig non-equivalent, and among L1_audited non-equivalent, with Wilson/bootstrap CIs;\\n  - the error-type distribution among L3-unfaithful items, weighted, with the scope + cardinality share explicitly;\\n  - solver-vs-panel agreement on the 50 equivalent items and on same_meaning;\\n  - Fleiss kappa (L3 all items; L0 on the 20% full-panel subset), per-member calibration accuracy, and the total $ per phase.\\n- README.md and .aii/manifest.yaml: keep data_out*, label_report.json, raw generations (raw/generations/*.jsonl — irreproducible API outputs) and cost_ledger.jsonl; delete raw/hf_downloads/ as redownloadable (with the source hf ids and URLs).\\n\\nFAILURE HANDLING.\\n- Budget pressure, cut in this order: samples to 350 sentences -> M1 swapped to claude-haiku-4.5 -> L3 to 200 (170 non-equiv + 30 equiv) -> E1 to 100. Never cut L0/L4.\\n- Solver slowness: label greedy candidates first; samples are best-effort (report coverage).\\n- If a panel member fails calibration twice, use a 2-member panel plus M1 on everything, and report it.\\n- If the FOLIO train mirror is unavailable, use the Yale-LILY GitHub v0.0 folio-train.jsonl.\\n- If the disjoint FOLIO pool has fewer than 250 eligible sentences, take all of them and back-fill from MALLS.\",", "+  \"dataset_search_plan\": \"Scale up in stages (aii-long-running-tasks): mini (20 sentences) -> 100 -> full. Where it differs from the original, run the bounded solver and set refined_differs = the result of a non-equivalence check.\\n  Use: an INDEPENDENT (non-panel) wrong-gold signal for the FOLIO-train held-out sentences AND for the L4 screen sentences (FOLIO validation). Do NOT use refined gold as the label reference, because its method is unknown; keep it as metadata.\\n  A sibling run found that 51.7% of FOLIO-train pairs are ground facts, 8.4% use ⊕, only 1 sentence has unless/except, and the median length is 9 words.
